## Recommendation: Pre and post construction: Bifenthrin 2.5% EC shall be applied at 0.05% a.i. conc. i.e. 20.0 ml formulated product diluted in 1 liter of water for the control of termites in building during pre and post construction. Treatment should be as per IS 6313 (Part 2):2001 for pre construction chemical treatment and IS 6313 (Part-3): 2001 for post construction treatment of the existing building. Recommendation for use of control of Wood borer (Powder Post Beetle) in plywood, veneer and wood — Use
